<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    let slideIndex = 1;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    showSlides(slideIndex);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    function plusSlides(n) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        showSlides(slideIndex += n);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    function showSlides(n) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let i;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let slides = document.getElementsByClassName(&quot;slideshow-item&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let dots = document.getElementsByClassName(&quot;dot&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        if (n &gt; slides.length) { slideIndex = 1 }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        if (n &lt; 1) { slideIndex = slides.length }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        for (i = 0; i &lt; slides.length; i++) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            slides[i].style.display = &quot;none&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        for (i = 0; i &lt; dots.length; i++) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            dots[i].className = dots[i].className.replace(&quot; active&quot;, &quot;&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        slides[slideIndex - 1].style.display = &quot;block&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        dots[slideIndex - 1].className += &quot; active&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>&lt;/script&gt;</del></span>

<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    let slideIndex = 1;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    showSlides(slideIndex);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    function plusSlides(n) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        showSlides(slideIndex += n);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    function showSlides(n) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let i;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let slides = document.getElementsByClassName(&quot;slideshow-item&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let dots = document.getElementsByClassName(&quot;dot&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        if (n &gt; slides.length) { slideIndex = 1 }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        if (n &lt; 1) { slideIndex = slides.length }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        for (i = 0; i &lt; slides.length; i++) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            slides[i].style.display = &quot;none&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        for (i = 0; i &lt; dots.length; i++) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            dots[i].className = dots[i].className.replace(&quot; active&quot;, &quot;&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        slides[slideIndex - 1].style.display = &quot;block&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        dots[slideIndex - 1].className += &quot; active&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>    }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>&lt;/script&gt;</del></span>
